The prompt is the same fewshot_v1 prompt plus a fixed block 'Use only these predicates:' listing each template atom as Name(arity): English gloss, taken from the lexicon. It has no hint about exception handling, and the few-shot examples are unchanged. The template's predicate signature is given ONLY so labels can be computed as pure z3 equivalence (a labelling device, not a grounded-vs-ungrounded comparison, which is out of scope). SIG labels: CORRECT = z3-equivalent to the weak OR the strong reading; ERROR = equivalent to neither and uses only signature symbols; OFF_SIGNATURE (introduces other symbols) = reported separately and excluded from the primary analysis; T8 converse = READING_CHOICE, excluded; z3 timeout = UNKNOWN, excluded but counted; unparseable = kept in the coverage view. No aligner, panel or LLM touches SIG labels. FREE keeps dataset 3's pre-registered solver-modulo-alignment labels, and its VOCAB_GRAN/COMPOUND rows go to dataset E's Haiku/GLM/Kimi panel prompt (capped at $1.5; not the failed R_ADJ adjudicator). GENERATION: the 10 slots (Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B, Qwen3-235B-A22B-2507, Mistral-Small-3.2-24B, DeepSeek-V3.2 reasoning off, Gemma-3-27B, Phi-4, GPT-4.1-mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash reasoning off, Command-R7B), temperature 0, 250 sentences x 2 conditions = about 5,000 calls. Estimate from a 20-sentence pilot first; expect about $2.5-3.5. Run SIG first, because it carries the confound-free test. If the key is down for good, the pre-declared fallback is >=5 open-weight families on the local A4500 GPU (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-3-12B, Phi-4 nf4). That pool is labelled R_COMP-LOCAL and reported, never merged. TESTABILITY is declared in testability.json BEFORE any metric is computed: >=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT rows over >=25 sentences per condition. If SIG falls short, generate the pre-registered 100-sentence reserve top-up once. If it is still short, SIG is declared untestable and the result is reported as such (no third fix). SCORING, after the testability freeze. Consensus, leave-one-family-out: in SIG, c_score_sig = 1 - the share of the other 9 families' SIG outputs that are exactly z3-equivalent (no aligner is needed: shared signature). In FREE: c_score_align, NF-anchored and PEER_HYB (per the strategy's shared definition), plus g_score from peer_text.graded_consensus. Comparators: judge_cheap_disg and judge_cheap_orig (flash-lite, rubric A, disguise via E's disguise.py) on every parseable row of both conditions (~$0.6); local Qwen3-8B disguised judge (GPU, secondary); frontier judge on a 150-row stratified SIG subsample (cap $1). ANALYSES: (1) criterion (c): on R_COMP-SIG, paired Delta c_score_sig - judge_cheap_disg, with a sentence-clustered bootstrap (B=2000) stratified by template_id; on R_COMP-FREE, the sign for c_score_align and PEER_HYB vs the judge; (2) per template and exception type, weak-vs-strong acceptance, and label counts; (3) complexity curves within R_COMP (words, n_conditions, nesting); (4) mechanism inputs for next iteration's join: per row, write the pairwise equivalence class id of every candidate in its sentence (SIG exact classes; FREE ALIGN/HYB classes), so e (error endorsement) and d (correct divergence) can be computed on a second population; (5) cost: generation $ per sentence per family, judge $ per row, z3 seconds. OUTPUT: rcomp_candidates.jsonl (every row: sentence_id, template_id, system, family, condition, candidate_fol, label, label_source, strata, z3 class ids, all scores), method_out.json (exp_gen_sol_out, predict_* oriented), testability.json, prereg_sig.json, cost ledger, README. Hard stop $9.5 total, including the resumed dataset-3 phases. NOTE ON INPUTS: iteration-1/2 experiment and evaluation artifacts cannot be declared as dependencies of this artifact type. Hard rule: if any step is 30 min behind, apply the fallback ladder (fallback_plan) rather than skipping analyses silently.\\n\\nSTEP 0 SETUP\\n- uv venv; deps: z3-solver, numpy, scipy, scikit-learn, pandas, loguru, aiohttp/openai, nltk, spacy(+en_core_web_sm if fol_triage needs it), statsmodels. NEVER copied: output, reference_fol, auto_label, equiv_status, repair_ops, repair_status, convention_flags, panel_*, final_label, error_ops, label_tier, label_source, addrop_only_suspect, solver_lenient_label, correct_not_equivalent, reading_choice, gold_audit_flag, reference_status, class_id, class_size. Assert no forbidden key present; write sha256 of E_blind.jsonl.\\n- make_label_view(rows) -> data/E_labels.jsonl (item_id + all label fields). This file is written by a separate function and is read ONLY by analyse_E.py after the prereg check. score_E.py imports nothing from it (enforced by a grep test in tests/test_isolation.py).\\n- Note: strata.n_quant/depth/n_conditions were computed from the reference formula; they are covariates for analysis only and are NOT fed to any metric (assert in peer_text_score signature).\\n- Screen view: screen items = exp C results/screen_items.json joined to screen_adjudicated_labels.json by item_id, plus exp A per_item.jsonl features. Define screen labels:\\n  solver_label = map(auto_label): CORRECT/EQ->CORRECT, VOCAB_GRAN->CORRECT, ERROR->ERROR, COMPOUND->ERROR, else None.\\n  adj_label = final_label if label_tier in {A,B} and final_label in {CORRECT,ERROR} and not reading_choice else None.\\n  AGREE set = items with solver_label == adj_label (both non-None). Log the 2x2 confusion and n per class per track. Expected ~250-350 items; if either class < 60, add exp A's own solver label as a second 'solver' vector (agree = all available solver vectors agree with adj) and log.\\n- Family map (pre-registered, vendor level, used for leave-one-FAMILY-out): meta={G1,G1b}, qwen={G2}, mistral={G3}, deepseek={G4}, google={G5 gemma, G8 gemini}, microsoft={G6}, openai={G7, F}, cohere={G9}; zero-shot and few-shot variants of a slot are the same family. Sensitivity map = the row's metadata_family field. malls_gpt4_gold and ccg2lambda: scored as candidates, NEVER peers. Screen: Logic-LM gpt-3.5/gpt-4/davinci-003 and P6 gpt-4.1-mini are family openai; track-H originals are family human; P5 gemini-2.5-flash-lite is google.\\n\\nSTEP 2 src/peer_text.py (reusable functions; each with a precise docstring stating inputs, outputs, what is measured, what it is blind to)\\n2a parse & units\\n  parse_fol(s) -> AST via labeller/fol.py (dataset E parser) ; returns None on failure (coverage failure, counted).\\n  claim_units(ast) -> list[Unit]: NNF; distribute top-level ∀ over ∧ (∀x(A∧B) => ∀xA, ∀xB); split top-level ∧; for each universal rule ∀x̄(R1∧..∧Rn → C1∧..∧Cm) emit one unit per consequent conjunct Cj as (restrictor set {Ri}, consequent Cj) serialized back to a closed formula ∀x̄(R1∧..∧Rn → Cj); existential claims ∃x̄(...) kept whole; ground atoms/literals are units. Cap 16 units (log if exceeded; keep first 16 in textual order). Each unit keeps its location (index path in the original AST) for DROP/ADD reporting.\\n2b signatures (name-free)\\n  symbol_signature(ast) -> per predicate/constant: arity; polarity profile over occurrences (DOWN/UP/MIXED counts, syntactic polarity after expanding ↔/⊕ into both directions, so these occurrences are MIXED; syntactic monotonicity is sound for semantic monotonicity, and z3 monotonicity from formula_role_profile is used only where already cached); quantifier profile of its argument variables (force ∀/∃, prenex block index normalized by n_blocks); argument-sharing neighbourhood = multiset of (arity, polarity, slot index) of predicates sharing a variable with it, hashed 1-WL style; for constants: multiset of (predicate-arity, slot) they fill.\\n  text_anchor(symbol, text) -> set of sentence token indices matched by the symbol's name words using exp A content_accounting's name_words/lex/word_match (lemma+WordNet). Used ONLY by NF-anchored.\\n2c name_free_align(cand_fol, peer_fol, text=None, variant='NF-pure'|'NF-anchored', k=5, tau=None) -> {'mappings': [σ...], 'best': σ, 'cost': c, 'n_verified': v, 'n_units': u}\\n  cost matrix per sort (predicates grouped by arity; constants separately); arity mismatch = +inf. Structural cost in [0,1] = 0.25*L1(polarity profiles)/2 + 0.25*|quant-profile diff| + 0.25*|block-index diff| + 0.25*(1-Jaccard(neighbour hashes)). NF-anchored adds anchor cost (1-Jaccard(anchors)) if both anchored, 0.5 if either unanchored, weight 1.0. Weights fixed a priori, NOT tuned.\\n  Unequal counts: pad with dummy rows/cols at cost 0.6 (an unmatched symbol stays a fresh, peer-prefixed symbol, so entailments involving it fail = ADD/DROP).\\n  k-best: scipy.optimize.linear_sum_assignment for the best; alternatives by Murty-lite (for each edge of the incumbent, forbid it and re-solve; keep distinct; take k cheapest).\\n  Verification: for each candidate mapping, count verified unit entailments in both directions (peer ⊨ cand-unit, cand ⊨ peer-unit) using entails() below. ACCEPT argmax n_verified among mappings with cost <= min_cost + tau; tau = inf for NF-pure, tau = 0.5 for NF-anchored (pre-registered; this window stops the verifier from re-selecting a role-reversing permutation that the text anchors reject, which is the over-alignment path for REV errors). Tie -> lower cost.\\n  Known blind spot, stated in docstring: NF-pure cannot see errors that are automorphisms of the formula structure (e.g. ∀x(B→A) vs ∀x(A→B) with A,B structurally symmetric); measured by the ROLE_PERMUTE probe.\\n2d entailment engine\\n  entails(premise_ast, unit_ast, timeout_ms=2000) -> True/False/UNKNOWN. First a finite-model refuter: evaluate on 64 random interpretations over domains of size 1..3 (vendored repair_census.ev with seeds) — any model with premise true and unit false => False without z3. Otherwise z3: premise ∧ ¬unit unsat => True; sat => False; unknown/timeout => UNKNOWN. Identical normalized strings => True (exp C gotcha). Pair cache keyed by (sha1(premise_str), sha1(unit_str), sha1(mapping)) in a shelve/sqlite file cache/entail.sqlite (sqlite, WAL mode, one writer per process chunk then merge) so rewrites, peers and k-subsamples reuse work.\\n2e graded_consensus(text, fol, peers, variant, return_codes=True) -> {g_score, support, coverage, unit_codes, n_units, n_unknown, n_peers_used, c_score_nf, medoid_ops}\\n  peers: list of peer formulas (already leave-one-family-out filtered by the caller; unparseable peers dropped and counted). If n_peers_used < 2 -> g_score=None, flag peer_unavailable.\\n  For each peer p: σ_p = name_free_align(fol, p).best; rename p into candidate vocabulary (unmapped peer symbols prefixed 'pp_').\\n  support = mean over candidate units u (excluding units UNKNOWN for all peers) of fraction of peers with σ_p(p) ⊨ u (UNKNOWN pairs excluded from that fraction).\\n  majority units: every peer unit v of p is 'majority' if it is entailed by >=50% of the OTHER peers (peer-peer alignments come from the same cache: on E every row is both candidate and peer). coverage = fraction of majority peer units (pooled over peers, deduplicated by z3 mutual entailment within the candidate's vocabulary) that fol entails.\\n  g_score = 1 - 2*support*coverage/(support+coverage) (=1 if both 0).\\n  c_score_nf = fraction of peers p with fol ≡ σ_p(p) (both directions) under the accepted mapping.\\n  unit_codes: unsupported candidate unit (support < 0.5) -> try in order: negation of u is entailed by >=50% peers -> NEG; u with one ∀<->∃ flip supported -> QUANT; u with one binary-atom argument permutation supported -> SWAP; else ADD. Uncovered majority unit -> DROP (with its location and its text anchor if any). Codes carry the unit string.\\n  medoid_ops: exp C repair_census.search between candidate and the peer medoid (peer with max c_score_nf agreement), 10 s budget; second readout only.\\n2f text side: re-export content_accounting (L2-bow), role_questionnaire (flash-lite, text only), l3_compare, l3_score from vendor_a unchanged; l1 = fol_lint, l2_role = role_accounting as columns only.\\n2g peer_text_score(text, fol, peers, frozen=prereg['fusion']) -> {p_error, flag, fired_signal (argmax standardized contribution among PEER/TEXT), unit_codes, g_score, c_score_nf, l2_bow, l3_z3, coverage_status}. Features z-scored with SCREEN means/sds; missing L3 -> z=0 + flag; peer_unavailable -> use frozen TEXT-only model (pre-registered fallback). Unparseable fol -> p_error=1.0, coverage_status='UNPARSEABLE'.\\n2h unit tests (tests/test_peer_text.py): identity (g=0), RENAME of all predicates (NF-pure g=0 exactly), dropped conjunct in restrictor -> DROP code at right location, ∀->∃ -> QUANT, negated consequent -> NEG, swapped binary args -> SWAP, added conjunct -> ADD, contrapositive/De Morgan/prenex rewrites -> g=0, identical-string cache returns True, timeout returns UNKNOWN not False.\\n\\nSTEP 3 LAUNCH E API JOBS EARLY (background, label-free; src/run_api_E.py, PID-managed)\\n- Cost estimate BEFORE launch (write results/cost_estimate.json): judge ~8,000 parseable LLM+gold rows x 2 (orig, disguised) x ~320 in / ~60 out tokens at flash-lite $0.10/$0.40 per M ≈ $0.9-1.2; L3 questionnaire 700 texts x 2 (orig, disguised) ≈ $0.25; total ≈ $1.4, cap $3.0.\\n- judge_cheap_orig and judge_cheap_disg: vendor_d.judges.judge_json with rubric=RUBRIC_A, the USER_JSON_A template (includes error_type), model google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite, params exactly as in exp D prereg.json; disguise via vendor_d.disguise.disguise_item(text, fol) (store the disguised pair in results/E_disguise_map.jsonl; also record agreement with E's own metadata disguise as a note only). Score = 1 - faithful_prob/100 (oriented to P(error)); keep verdict and error_type. Concurrency 16, retries 2, cache vendored. Order rows CTRL, EXC, L20, L25 so partial runs are still usable.\\n- L3: role_questionnaire on 700 E texts and 700 disguised texts (disguised text uses exp D disguise; the z3 side then uses the disguised formula from the same disguise_item call).\\n- Skip unparseable rows for the judge (their score is set to 1.0 = flagged, like every metric); log count.\\n\\nSTEP 4 SCREEN: COMPUTE, SELECT, FIT, FREEZE (src/screen_fit.py)\\n4a Peer pools on screen: per sentence from exp C peer_outputs.jsonl (P1-P6) + Logic-LM systems. PRIMARY pool = leave-one-FAMILY-out, so track-L (OpenAI) candidates get P1-P5 and H candidates get P1-P6 (+ Logic-LM rows where present). SENSITIVITY pool = direction's '6 fresh + 2 other Logic-LM'.\\n4b Regression check: vendored content_accounting/l3_score on screen reproduce exp A per_item.jsonl values on >=98% of items (log diffs); c_score_align reproduces exp C's c_score AUROC 0.866 +-0.005 on track L.\\n4c Probes (sha1-seeded, built from screen items whose adj_label = CORRECT and track L/H, pre-registered BEFORE any variant is scored):\\n  MEANING_RENAME: 150 items; replace one predicate by a different same-arity predicate from the same story (story_premises_fol / declared_predicates in invariance_items or exp A story data); verify non-equivalence by z3 under identity mapping; ERROR by construction.\\n  ROLE_PERMUTE: 150 items; swap two same-arity predicates occupying different polarity/role positions; keep only z3-non-equivalent; ERROR by construction.\\n  SWAP: 150 items; permute args of one binary atom; keep z3-non-equivalent.\\n  RENAME set: exp D invariance_items family RENAME (if absent or <40 items, generate with vendor_c rewrites.rw_rename + WordNet, bases = the same base_item_ids).\\n4d Score g_score/c_score_nf for NF-pure and NF-anchored on: AGREE set, rewrite set, probes.\\n4e VARIANT SELECTION RULE (written to prereg_selection.json with sha256 BEFORE 4d runs): component threshold per variant = g_score value giving FA 0.10 on AGREE-set CORRECT items from LLM systems; eligible if RENAME FA <= 0.10 (fraction of RENAME rewrites of CORRECT bases flagged) AND ROLE_PERMUTE recall >= 0.50; choose the eligible variant with the highest AGREE-set AUROC of g_score; tie (|Δ|<0.005) -> NF-anchored. If none eligible -> F1 declared FAILED: use c_score_align-based graded consensus (iteration-1 aligner inside graded_consensus) as the peer signal and report it.\\n4f Fusion (F3): features [g_score, c_score_nf, l2_bow, l3_z3] on AGREE set (tracks L+H), standardized with AGREE-set stats; sklearn LogisticRegression(l2), C in {0.1,1,10} chosen by 5-fold GroupKFold on sentence_id (metric AUROC); final fit on all AGREE items. Threshold = score giving FA = 0.10 on AGREE CORRECT items from LLM systems (track L only; H originals excluded from threshold). Also fit and freeze: PEER-only (g_score, threshold at FA 0.10), TEXT-only logistic [l2_bow, l3_z3], and SENSITIVITY fusion on ALL screen items with adj_label (tiers A+B). Report screen OOF AUROCs (grouped CV) for all, and screen-side P1/P2 pre-test numbers as descriptive.\\n4g PREREG FREEZE: results/prereg.json containing: vendored file sha1s; E_blind sha256; family maps; unit/cap/timeouts (2 s, k=5, tau); variant selected + selection evidence; fusion coefficients/intercept/means/sds/C/threshold; PEER, TEXT, SENSITIVITY models; label-regime definitions (R_AB, R_A below); strata testability (copy dataset card table: pooled, L25, L20, EXC, CTRL for A+B; R_A testable L20, EXC, pooled L20+EXC; sign-only elsewhere; any cell <50/50 declared untestable); every analysis (a)-(j) with its statistic, B=2000 cluster bootstrap by sentence_id, seed 20260923; P1-P4 decision rules; success rule for criterion (a)-R_AB/R_A and (c)-long; the k=6 pool-size check. Write results/prereg.sha256 = sha256(prereg.json) and UTC timestamp. Commit to git.\\n\\nSTEP 5 SCORE E (src/score_E.py; reads ONLY data/E_blind.jsonl + prereg.json)\\n- Per sentence: parse all rows; pool = parseable LLM rows of other families (vendor map). Tasks = unique (cand_str, peer_str) ordered pairs per sentence (dedupe identical normalized formulas first). ProcessPoolExecutor(7 workers, spawn), chunk by sentence, SIGALRM per-pair 30 s hard cap (pair -> UNKNOWN), RLIMIT_AS 3.5 GB per worker. Order strata CTRL, EXC, L20, L25; mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> all; after mini and 100, log sec/sentence per stratum and extrapolate; if projected > 100 min for the rest, apply fallback ladder step 1 (k=2, timeout 1 s) BEFORE continuing, and record the change in results/deviations.json.\\n- Compute per row: g_score, support, coverage, unit_codes, n_unknown, n_peers_used, c_score_nf, c_score_align (vendor_c.score_candidate with the same LOFO pool), medoid_ops, and g_score_k6 (mean over 5 seeded random subsamples of 6 peers; = full when pool <=6), g_score_famfield (sensitivity family map).\\n- Text side per row: l2_bow (content_accounting), l3_z3 (l3_compare against the cached questionnaire for its sentence), l1 codes, l2_role; the same on disguised text/formula for l3 (contamination).\\n- Fused p (primary), PEER, TEXT, SENSITIVITY fusion, fired_signal, flag at frozen threshold; timing (cpu s per row) and $ per row (L3 cost / rows per sentence + 0 for z3).\\n- Write results/per_item_E.jsonl (one line per E item_id: every score above + judge_cheap_orig/disg scores, verdicts, error_type, costs, seconds, coverage_status, fold_E = int(sha1('E_folds_v1|'+sentence_id),16) % 5 (same as sibling EXP2), prereg_sha256). File mtime must be later than prereg.sha256.\\n- Rewrite set: score all 282(+RENAME) rewrites and the 3 probes with the frozen pipeline (screen pools) -> results/rewrite_scores_pt.jsonl.\\n\\nSTEP 6 ANALYSE (src/analyse_E.py)\\n- Guard: refuse unless results/prereg.sha256 exists, matches sha256(prereg.json), and its mtime < mtime(per_item_E.jsonl); then load E_labels.jsonl.\\n- Label regimes: R_AB = system_class llm, label_tier in {A,B}, final_label in {CORRECT,ERROR}, not reading_choice (CONTESTED excluded; sensitivities CONTESTED->CORRECT and ->ERROR; with/without L25 top-up; malls_gpt4_gold rows as a separate 'gold-as-system' table). R_A = same with label_tier == A.\\n- Helpers: auroc (sklearn), auprc, prec/rec/FA at frozen threshold with prevalence, cluster bootstrap by sentence_id (B=2000) returning CI for each metric and for paired differences on IDENTICAL item sets (all metrics must be non-missing: missing is imputed per the prereg rule, never dropped; assert n equal across metrics), DeLong as secondary.\\n(a) Headline: Δ AUROC = PEER+TEXT - judge_cheap_disg; pooled R_AB, pooled R_A (L20+EXC), per stratum (L25/L20/EXC/CTRL), long pool (L25+L20+EXC). Also PEER, TEXT, g_score, c_score_nf, c_score_align, l2_bow, l3_z3, judge_orig, L1, L2-role in the same table. Criterion (a)-this-artifact: CI>0 pooled R_AB AND sign>0 pooled R_A. Criterion (c)-long: Δ CI>0 in long pool (R_COMP half is iteration 3).\\n(b) P1 crossover: per metric, threshold at matched FA 0.10 and 0.20 computed on the regime's CORRECT items; recall per error group: STRUCT group = tier-A errors whose repair_ops ⊆ {QUANT,SCOPE,BIND,SWAP,NEG,REV,RESTR} with <=2 ops; COVERAGE group = ops ⊆ {ADD,DROP}; MEANING_RENAME = tier-B errors with error_ops containing MEANING_RENAME; PEER_ENDORSED = errors whose candidate ≡_NF the peer medoid. Recall differences PEER-TEXT per group with cluster-bootstrap CI. CONFIRMED if PEER>TEXT on STRUCT and TEXT>PEER on MEANING_RENAME∪PEER_ENDORSED with at least one CI excluding 0 and no reversal with CI excluding 0; REFUTED if either reverses with CI excluding 0; else INCONCLUSIVE. Judge recall shown alongside.\\n(c) P2: Spearman(g_score, TEXT) among true errors (CI); fusion-gain decomposition: AUROC = Σ_g (n_g/n_err)·AUROC_g over error groups {peer-endorsed, non-endorsed} vs all CORRECT; share of (fused - best single) gain from peer-endorsed. CONFIRMED if rho < 0.4 AND share >= 0.5.\\n(d) P3: per metric logistic y ~ z(s) + z(words) + z(s)·z(words) with sentence-cluster bootstrap CI on the interaction, for c_score_nf, c_score_align, g_score, fused, judge_disg; also AUROC by word bin (<15, 15-19, 20-24, 25-29, >=30) and fused-minus-judge Δ per stratum plus difference-in-Δ (long pool minus CTRL) with CI. CONFIRMED if c_score_nf interaction < 0 (CI), |g| <= 0.5|c_score_nf| and diff-in-Δ > 0.\\n(e) P4: on the shared rewrite set, per family (REPRINT, RENAME, REORDER, DEMORGAN, PRENEX, CONTRAPOS, plus whatever families exp D used): FA on rewrites of CORRECT bases and flip rate vs base flag, for g_score, c_score_nf, c_score_align, l2_bow, l3, fused; judge rows read from exp D rewrite_scores.jsonl. CONFIRMED if fused FA <= 0.10 on EVERY family and a judge >= 0.25 on >= 1 family. Probes: recall on MEANING_RENAME, ROLE_PERMUTE, SWAP per metric (descriptive, synthetic, never mixed with real-error AUROC).\\n(f) Error-type readout: on R_A errors with 1-2 repair_ops, accuracy = the row's highest-priority unit_code (NEG > QUANT > SWAP > DROP > ADD) is contained in repair_ops, exact code match only (REV/RESTR/MOVE/CONN/SCOPE/BIND have no unit code and count as misses; report the per-code confusion matrix and the same accuracy restricted to errors whose ops are all codable), medoid_ops agreement, judge error_type agreement, and a chance baseline = expected accuracy of drawing codes from the marginal code distribution. Also the census by-product: share of polarity-type vs coverage-type ops among R_A errors (descriptive).\\n(g) System-level: 13 system x variant rows (LLM systems); per row mean fused p and mean judge score vs R_AB error rate; Kendall tau-b with sentence-bootstrap CI; also 9-family averaged version. Descriptive only.\\n(h) Complexity: AUROC and FA@threshold vs words, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, exception_type (bins with >=30/30 else marked untestable), for fused, PEER, TEXT, judge_disg.\\n(i) Contamination: AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disg) for judge and for l3 (paired bootstrap); DiD fused-vs-judge; MDE = 2.8 x bootstrap SE stated next to each.\\n(j) Coverage (every E row in denominator: parse fail, peer_unavailable, n_unknown>0 share, L3 failures) and cost: $ per item (API) and cpu-seconds per item per component; per-stratum medians.\\n(k) Also: gold-as-system rows (malls_gpt4_gold) flag rate vs panel gold-audit, correct-but-not-equivalent rows' flag rate (FA on tier-B CORRECT separately from tier-A CORRECT: tests whether PEER+TEXT penalises vocabulary/granularity).\\n- Write results/analysis.json, results/tables.md, results/p_tests.json (each P with CONFIRMED/REFUTED/INCONCLUSIVE), results/deviations.json.\\n\\nSTEP 7 OUTPUTS\\n- method_out.json (exp_gen_sol_out schema via aii-json): one example per E row: input = the blind view JSON, output = final_label (added only after analysis), predict_peer_text (p), predict_peer, predict_text, predict_g_score, predict_c_score_nf, predict_c_score_align, predict_l2_bow, predict_l3, predict_judge_cheap_disg, predict_judge_cheap_orig, metadata_unit_codes, metadata_fired_signal, metadata_n_unknown, metadata_cost_usd, metadata_secs. full/mini/preview variants; split with aii-file-size-limit if needed.\\n- README.md (layout, how to run, results headline, workspace paths), .aii/manifest.yaml (delete: .venv/ regenerable 'uv sync'; cache/entail.sqlite regenerable if >10MB 'uv run src/score_E.py'; data/nltk_data redownloadable 'python -m nltk.downloader wordnet omw-1.4'; keep: results/, data/E_blind.jsonl, prereg).\",\n\"fallback_plan\": \"FALLBACK LADDER (apply in order, log each step in results/deviations.json; never silent). (1) z3 too slow (projected E scoring > 100 min after the 100-sentence stage): k-best 5->2, per-check timeout 2 s->1 s, unit cap 16->10, coverage majority computed against the 6 lowest-cost peers only; if still too slow, finish CTRL, EXC, L20 fully and score L25 on the original-200 sentences only (the top-up is untestable alone anyway), reporting the L25 cut. (2) Finite-model prefilter disagrees with z3 on >1% of a 500-pair audit (a prefilter 'False' where z3 says True cannot happen if ev is correct; if it does, the ev port is buggy): disable the prefilter for the affected construct (↔/⊕/equality) and use z3 only. (3) F1 fails the selection gate (no variant has RENAME FA <= 0.10 and ROLE_PERMUTE recall >= 0.50): declare F1 FAILED, run graded consensus with the iteration-1 aligner (vendor_c align) as the peer signal, keep NF numbers as a reported negative, and state that the align() instrument is shared with the labeller for this run (the confound stays open). (4) AGREE set too small (<60 per class): add exp A's solver label vector; if still <60, fit fusion on all adjudicated screen items (the SENSITIVITY model becomes primary) and state that F3's confound control failed. (5) g_score degenerate on screen (AUROC < 0.6 or >40% ties at 0): fuse c_score_nf instead of g_score (pre-registered alternative feature set [c_score_nf, l2_bow, l3_z3]) and report F2 as failed. (6) OpenRouter key exhausted / 402 / daily limit: check GET /api/v1/key; the judge is required for the headline, so wait for reset only if the run window allows; otherwise run the judge on a pre-registered stratified subsample (sha1 order within stratum, all of R_A rows first, then R_AB rows, until budget) and restrict every paired comparison to that subsample; L3 questionnaire falls back to exp A's local Qwen2.5-1.5B path ONLY as a labelled robustness row (the primary L3 must stay flash-lite for continuity with the screen fit). Never switch the judge model: the bar is flash-lite disguised. (7) Budget: hard stop at $3.0; if cost_estimate after the first 500 judge calls projects > $2.5 for the judge, drop judge_cheap_orig on CTRL first (contamination then reported on MALLS strata only). (8) E file layout differs (directory of parts, missing keys): glob both layouts; if a label field name differs, map it in data_views.py and log the mapping; never read label fields in score_E.py. (9) Parser disagreement: if our parser parses a row E marks UNPARSEABLE (or vice versa) on >1% rows, keep E's label for coverage accounting and score what parses; report the mismatch count. (10) If the confirmation fails (CI includes 0 / negative), do NOT tune or subgroup-hunt: report DISCONFIRM for this regime with the crossover (P1) table as the finding, exactly as pre-registered.\",\n\"testing_plan\": \"UNIT (before any data run, tests/): (1) test_peer_text.py cases listed in STEP 2h, including exact g=0 under full predicate+constant renaming for NF-pure, DROP location correctness on a 3-condition restrictor, QUANT/NEG/SWAP/ADD codes, z3-equivalent rewrites (contrapositive, De Morgan, prenex, reorder) giving g=0 and c_score_nf=0, UNKNOWN on a forced 1 ms timeout, identical-string cache hit = True. (2) test_isolation.py: E_blind.jsonl contains none of the forbidden keys; score_E.py source does not reference E_labels or any label key (grep); analyse_E.py raises when prereg.sha256 is missing, mismatched, or newer than per_item_E.jsonl. (3) Prefilter audit: 500 random (premise, unit) pairs from screen; prefilter 'False' must never coincide with z3 'True' (0 tolerance); report prefilter hit rate and speedup. REGRESSION on the screen: vendored L2-bow/L3 reproduce exp A per_item.jsonl (>=98% identical); vendored c_score_align reproduces exp C track-L AUROC 0.866 +-0.005; judge re-scoring of 30 screen items hits the vendored cache (0 new cost) and reproduces exp D scores exactly (proves prompt/disguise identity). SANITY SIGNALS before the full E run: on the screen AGREE set, NF variants should give AUROC in the 0.75-0.90 range (iter-1 exact consensus 0.87 on own labels); PEER vs TEXT Spearman among errors should be clearly < 1; placebo (shuffled labels within sentence) AUROC ~0.5 for fused. STAGED SCALE on E: mini = 20 sentences (5 per stratum) — check per-row outputs are complete, n_unknown share (<5% expected on CTRL, report on L25), peers per row distribution (top-up rows 3-4 peers), seconds per sentence per stratum, API cost per row; then 100 sentences; then all. JUDGE sanity: after the first 200 E judge calls, parse-failure rate < 2% and mean cost per call within 30% of the estimate, else stop and fix. POST-HOC AUDIT (after analysis): tests/audit_rederive.py recomputes the pooled R_AB AUROCs and the headline Δ from per_item_E.jsonl + E_labels.jsonl through a separate minimal code path (sklearn only) and asserts agreement to 1e-9; permutation placebo (labels shuffled within stratum) gives ~0.5 for all metrics; confirm every paired comparison used identical n.\"\n,\n\"domain_practice\": \"What I read: the neurosymbolic field handbook (aii-handbook-auto-neurosymbolic: never score on FOLIO/MALLS gold as shipped, ~36-39% wrong [2606.02837]; compile/provable is not faithfulness; gold-free round-trip certification is an occupied lane [2604.25031] and may only be a baseline); dataset E's card (label rule, tiers, testability table, panel accuracy, correct-but-not-equivalent rates); iteration-1 exp A/C/D code and READMEs (prereg, judges.py rubric, disguise, invariance set, consensus.py). I did NOT do fresh web lookups in this step (time budget); the metric-meta-evaluation norms below are the ones the strategist cited from standing knowledge (TRUE, SummaC, FRANK, Goyal & Durrett, WMT metrics tasks, Deutsch et al. 2021/2023) and are treated as the reference practice. BASELINES a reviewer names first: an LLM-as-judge on the same items (here the disguised cheap judge, the pre-registered incumbent at 0.777 on the screen), then a stronger/frontier judge, sampling self-consistency (SelfCheckGPT/SAC3-style consistency; SAC3 is the prior for cross-model agreement), round-trip NLI/embedding similarity, parse rate, and the best cross-fitted combination of baselines (the 'is it just re-weighting' test). Fair tuning = baselines get the same prompt-selection budget; here the judge prompt is frozen from iteration 1 (not tuned on E) and our metric is also frozen on the screen, so neither side is tuned on the confirmation set. DATA: real outputs of several systems on held-out inputs with adjudicated labels (TRUE/SummaC/FRANK pattern); for NL->FOL, corrected FOLIO/MALLS (DSAVlab) and MALLS long sentences re-audited; original FOLIO/MALLS gold is known-noisy, and short FOLIO sentences saturate (track L is 485/524 items < 12 words). CONTROLS: identical items and label vector for every metric; unparseable outputs in the denominator; sentence-clustered resampling (up to 14 systems per sentence); contamination check by entity/predicate disguise; family-disjointness between adjudicator, judge and generators (self-preference: Gemini is both a generator (G8) and the judge family). The confound this design is most likely to be caught on: shared instruments between labeller and metric (align() shared with the solver labeller; lexical sensitivity shared with the panel). HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH: item-level ROC AUC with 95% bootstrap CIs (B>=1000, cluster by input), paired differences on the same items; ~300+ per class for pooled comparisons (SE ~0.02, paired MDE ~0.04-0.05), >=50/50 per reported stratum, else declared untestable before scoring; system-level correlations need many systems (WMT uses ~15-40 systems) and with 13 system-variants are descriptive with CIs. MEASURES/REPORTING: ROC AUC primary (threshold-free, TRUE convention), AUPRC with prevalence, precision/recall/FA at a threshold fixed on dev data, Kendall tau (tie-aware, tau-b) at system level with bootstrap CIs, per-error-type recall (FRANK), invariance to meaning-preserving rewrites, cost per item, and results across two label protocols (the MQM vs DA lesson: a win on one label protocol is not believed until it holds on another).\",\n\"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS: (1) item-level ROC AUC with sentence-clustered paired bootstrap on identical item sets for every metric, with DeLong as secondary; (2) frozen-on-dev / scored-once-on-held-out, enforced mechanically (label-stripped allowlist view, prereg sha256 older than the score file, analyse_E refuses otherwise); (3) the incumbent judge is the exact iteration-1 prompt and disguise, so the comparison is against a baseline tuned no less than our metric; (4) unparseable rows stay in the denominator in a coverage table and in coverage-inclusive AUROC; (5) contamination via disguise for every LLM component, with MDE stated; (6) two label protocols (R_AB panel-adjudicated, R_A solver-only tier A) with the regime shift itself reported; (7) testability declared before scoring from the dataset card counts (pooled A+B ~864 CORRECT / 1,822 ERROR: paired MDE ~0.04; R_A only L20+EXC pooled 188/261); (8) per-error-type recall, rewrite invariance on ONE shared rewrite set, cost and complexity curves; (9) instrument-disjointness: F1 removes the shared align() from the peer signal, F3 fits weights only where the solver label and the panel label agree, and c_score_align is kept as a reference column so the effect of removing the shared aligner is visible; (10) self-preference check: judge AUROC is also reported excluding Google-family generator rows (G5, G8). DEPARTS: (a) Only two of four label regimes are scored here (R_ADJ and R_COMP are built by sibling dataset artifacts this iteration and joined in iteration 3); cost: the claim's headline criterion (a) is only half-tested in this artifact, and R_A is testable only in L20+EXC, so the long-sentence claim under a panel-independent label waits for R_COMP. (b) The panel labels (R_AB) are STRICT (accepts 0.61 of expert-correct formulas, majority accuracy 0.727 on real errors), and the TEXT signal shares lexical sensitivity with the panel; cost: an R_AB win for TEXT-heavy fusion is partly circular. Mitigation inside budget: R_A (solver-only) reported with the same weight, the tier-B-CORRECT FA reported separately, and weights fitted only on label-agreeing items. (c) Nested cross-fitted ΔAUROC over S4 (criterion b) and the frontier-judge ratio (criterion c second half) are not computed here (EXP2 owns S4/folds_E/frontier); cost: 'adds signal over the best baseline combination' is not established by this artifact alone; the per-item file is produced so iteration 3 can do it on identical folds. (d) System-level tau over 13 system-variants from one prompt at temperature 0 is below what WMT would accept; reported as descriptive with CI only. (e) Screen-to-E distribution shift: screen pools have ~5 peers and short sentences, E has ~8-11 peers and long sentences, so frozen standardisation may be mis-scaled; mitigated by the pre-registered k=6 subsampled g_score and by AUROC being scale-free (the threshold-based FA/precision numbers carry this risk and are flagged as such). (f) Synthetic probes (MEANING_RENAME, ROLE_PERMUTE, SWAP) are used only as a component-selection guard and descriptive per-type sensitivity, never as the faithfulness target, in line with the user's requirement. (g) No fresh literature lookups in this planning step; norms cited from the handbook and the strategist's standing-knowledge list, so any reporting convention newer than those sources may be missing.\"\n}\n", "numLines": 13, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 13}}

Make a 1-token probe call and GET /key to log limit_remaining. If the key is at its daily limit, do all CPU/GPU work first and poll every 15 min until 00:00 UTC.\n- Budget: vendored budget.py (reserve cost inside the semaphore). Hard cap $2.0, soft stop $1.6. results/cost_ledger.jsonl gets $ and seconds for every call.\n- Check the prereg_baselines.json R_AB/R_A label-vector sha1s against dataset E; stop on mismatch.\n\nSTEP 1 PAIR ENGINE (src/pairs.py; the core of HYB)\n- pair_verdicts(cand_s, peer_s, text, ent) -> {align_eq, nf_eq, hyb_eq, nf_cost, nf_rank, align_map, nf_map, secs}.\n  align_eq = vendor_c common.eqmv(parse(peer), parse(cand)) (EXACTLY the call pool_scoring.score_sentence makes for c_score_align, with the 30 s SIGALRM cap -> None).\n  nf_eq = peer_text.pair_result(cand_s, peer_s, ent, text=text, variant='NF-anchored', k=5, tau=0.5)['equiv'].\n  hyb_eq = True if align_eq is True or nf_eq is True; False if both are False; None (UNKNOWN) otherwise. UNKNOWN never counts as agreement.\n- Pair cache: sqlite (WAL) keyed by (sha1(cand_s), sha1(peer_s), mode, text_sha1 for NF). Identical canonical strings -> True in every mode (exp C gotcha).\n- Scores, leave-one-FAMILY-out with exp 5's vendor family map (malls_gpt4_gold and ccg2lambda are never peers; unparseable peers are dropped and counted):\n  c_score_m = 1 - (#peers with m_eq True)/(#peers used), for m in {align, nf, hyb}; NA if fewer than 2 peers (flag peer_unavailable).\n  g_score_hyb (pre-registered): graded_consensus where each peer's mapping is the ALIGN map if align_eq is True, else the NF-anchored map if nf_eq is True, else whichever of the two has more verified unit entailments (ties -> ALIGN). All other graded_consensus internals are unchanged.\n- ProcessPoolExecutor (spawn, workers = usable vCPUs - 1; RLIMIT_AS 3.5 GB per worker), chunked by sentence. Scale mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> all, logging sec/sentence per stratum and extrapolating (aii-long-running-tasks). If the projected E time is > 120 min, cap NF k 5 -> 3 (a deviation, logged).\n\nSTEP 2 PART A: DEVELOPMENT SCORING (label-free up to the freeze)\n2a Screen L+H with exp C peers (P1-P6 + Logic-LM; track-L OpenAI candidates get P1-P5, as in exp 5): c/g for align, nf and hyb.\n2b E: every E row (LLM + gold + ccg as candidates). The scoring job reads the E BLIND view only (exp 5 data_views allowlist; label keys are absent, asserted).\n2c REGRESSION GATE (must pass before any use): re-derived c_score_align == frozen per_item_E c_score_align, and re-derived c_score_nf == frozen nf_c_score, on >= 99% of rows (|diff| < 1e-9; UNKNOWN-pattern differences listed). The frozen columns stay the headline values; recomputed ones are used only for HYB. On a > 1% mismatch, stop and diagnose (parser version, timeouts, family map); do not proceed on unmatched code.\n2d Rewrites: the exp D 282 shared rewrites (screen pools) and PERTURB's RENAME_SYN / RENAME_NONCE controls (E / R_COMP pools, Step 4), all modes.\n2e Probes: exp 5 screen_probes.json MEANING_RENAME, ROLE_PERMUTE and SWAP, all modes.\n\nSTEP 3 PREREG_HYB FREEZE (results/prereg_hyb.json + sha256 + UTC time + git commit), written BEFORE E labels are joined and before any PERTURB label-based statistic. It contains:\n- the pair definitions above and g_score_hyb's mapping rule;\n- THRESHOLD RULE: per variant, the matched-FA threshold at FA = 0.10 on screen LLM CORRECT items (exp 5 AGREE-set rule) WITH FRACTIONAL tie-breaking. At the tie level t, flag a row with score > t, plus a fraction q of rows with score == t, where q makes FA exactly 0.10. Expected-value FA/recall are reported as tie-aware rates, and no random draw is made.\n- the RENAME FA definition: share of rename-rewritten CORRECT bases flagged, computed separately on (i) exp D RENAME, (ii) PERTURB RENAME_SYN and (iii) PERTURB RENAME_NONCE. Flip rate is reported in its own column and never enters the rule.\n- the SELECTION RULE, verbatim from the strategy: among {HYB, NF-anchored}, a variant is eligible iff RENAME FA <= 0.10 on (i), (ii) and (iii). Among eligible variants, FREEZE the one with the higher E stratified AUROC under R_AB. Tie (|diff| < 0.005) -> NF-anchored, the single-map variant with fewer agreements, so less over-alignment risk. If neither is eligible: no rename-invariant consensus exists here; report both as failed and state that the R_COMP read uses ALIGN.\n- the bootstrap: B=2000, seed 0, clustered by sentence_id (E, screen) or base_id (PERTURB); strata definitions; testability thresholds (>= 50/50 per cell, else 'untestable' before scoring).\n\nSTEP 4 PART A ANALYSIS (src/analyse_hyb.py; refuses to run unless prereg_hyb.sha256 exists and predates the label join)\n- E R_AB and R_A (L20+EXC): AUROC, AUPRC and stratified AUROC for c_align (frozen), c_nf (frozen), c_hyb, g_hyb, g (frozen), nf_g (frozen), p_peer_text, judge_local_qwen8b_disg, and judge_cheap_disg where exp 5 rows exist. Paired stratified Deltas with CIs: HYB - ALIGN (target within 0.01), HYB - NF, HYB - judge_local, frozen-variant - ALIGN; pooled plus long pool plus per stratum. COVERAGE VIEW: unparseable = ERROR.\n- invariance_table.csv: rows = metric x rewrite family (REPRINT, RENAME, REORDER, DEMORGAN, CONTRAPOSITIVE, PRENEX from exp D; RENAME_SYN, RENAME_NONCE, CONTRAPOSITIVE, REORDER_COMMUTE, REORDER_QUANT, DEMORGAN from PERTURB). Columns: n_bases, FA_on_correct_bases, FA CI, flip_rate, n_flipped, source. FA and flip are in separate columns and never mixed.\n- Over-alignment: recall at the frozen threshold on PERTURB MEANING_RENAME (DOWN/UP), PERTURB SWAP, and screen ROLE_PERMUTE / MEANING_RENAME / SWAP probes, for ALIGN, NF and HYB.\n- NEW-AGREEMENT AUDIT on E: pairs where hyb_eq is True but align_eq is not. Report the count; the share where the candidate is a panel ERROR; and, the key precision readout, the LABEL-DISCORDANCE rate (one side CORRECT, the other ERROR under R_AB). Compare with the discordance rate among ALIGN-agreeing pairs. A higher discordance for new agreements = over-alignment, quantified on real outputs. Also draw a 30-pair sha1-ordered sample for a by-hand table in the README (candidate, peer, mapping, labels).\n- Length: AUROC by word bin and n_conditions for ALIGN / NF / HYB (does the NF path cost more on long sentences?).\n- Write per_item_hyb_E.jsonl (row_key, all modes, n_peers, n_unknown, fold_E), per_item_screen_hyb.jsonl, pairs_E.jsonl (sentence_id, cand row_key, peer row_key, align_eq, nf_eq, hyb_eq, nf_cost), selection.json (evidence plus the frozen variant).\n\nSTEP 5 PART B: PERTURB PER-TYPE SENSITIVITY (src/perturb_score.py, src/analyse_perturb.py)\n5a Rows: 4,234 mutants (ERROR) + 868 controls (CORRECT) + the 300 unmutated bases (CORRECT; control type BASE). The BASE control measures whether peers endorse the reference at all.\n5b Peers: for the 200 E bases, all parseable real LLM outputs of that E sentence (every family; the base is a reference with no family, so no family is left out). For the 100 R_COMP bases: consensus = NA (flagged 'no peers yet'), unless the sibling T2's rcomp_candidates.jsonl already exists at run time, in which case its FREE-condition outputs are used as a labelled secondary row. PERTURB is never scored against its own base.\n5c Metrics scored on every row where a scorer exists without new development (scored / unscorable counts and reasons go into coverage_perturb.csv):\n  consensus: c_align, c_nf, c_hyb, g, nf_g, g_hyb (Step 1 engine);\n  parse_ok; pilot structural metrics (exp 6 pilot_metrics.py; reference rows);\n  L2-bow (exp 5 l2_bow); L3 (l3_z3 against the cached E_l3_q questionnaire of the base sentence; R_COMP bases need about 100 new flash-lite questionnaires, ~$0.02, else NA);\n  PEER+TEXT frozen (peer_text_score with exp 5 prereg fusion);\n  SC-5 local (the mutant scored against exp 6's cached sc5_local_samples for its sentence with exp C's SC scorer; E bases only);\n  local round-trip NLI + embedding (exp 6 roundtrip.py, local verbaliser, GPU);\n  S4_local: exp 6 S4 feature set with ONE full-E fit (fit on all R_AB rows, coefficients saved to s4_perturb_coefs.json before PERTURB labels are read). A labelled deviation, since OOF folds do not exist for PERTURB rows.\n  judges: local Qwen3-8B disguised and local Llama-3.1-8B disguised (GPU, exp 6 local_llm prompts); judge_cheap_disg = gemini-2.5-flash-lite, rubric A (exp D judges.py verbatim), disguise = dataset 3's per-sentence bijection. About 5.4k calls, ~$0.35, cap $1.0. Estimate from a 20-row pilot first. Order: E-base rows first, then R_COMP-base rows; the type label is kept for Part C.\n5d Thresholds, frozen in prereg_perturb.json before any PERTURB statistic: PRIMARY = each metric's matched FA = 0.10 threshold on E R_AB LLM CORRECT rows (with fractional ties). This is uniform because every metric has E scores in per_item_E / E_baseline_features / Step 2; for the consensus variants the Step 3 screen thresholds are also reported. SECONDARY = FA 0.10 on the PERTURB non-rename controls (in-sample and labelled as such).\n5e Statistics per operator x polarity (QUANT/REV/RESTR are UP-only; SCOPE/UNGLUE are reported as absent with 0 rows; MOVE (6) is reported and not analysed):\n  recall at the frozen threshold, with a base-clustered CI;\n  BASE-MATCHED AUROC = mutants of the operator vs controls (non-rename controls + BASE) from the SAME bases, averaged over bases (within-base AUROC), plus the pooled version;\n  DOWN-vs-UP on the 1,354 matched pairs: the paired detection difference (flag_DOWN - flag_UP), its base-clustered bootstrap CI and McNemar counts, plus the paired score difference. Prediction written in the prereg: equivalence-based consensus is polarity-symmetric (|diff| < 0.05), while lexical, NLI and judge metrics may miss DOWN (restrictor-side) edits more often;\n  breakdowns by base reference_status (TRUSTED_AGREED / GOLD_PANEL_OK / PANEL_REPAIRED / R_COMP) AND by BASE ENDORSEMENT (the base's own c_hyb < 1, i.e. at least one peer agrees, vs no peer agrees). Consensus can only detect a mutant of a base that peers endorse, so this split is the correct denominator for interpreting consensus recall. Report both, and the share of bases endorsed;\n  INVARIANCE/SENSITIVITY TRADE-OFF per metric: the point (RENAME_SYN FA, MEANING_RENAME recall), plus the same with RENAME_NONCE, in one table and one figure spec. A metric must be low on the first axis and high on the second;\n  ADD split by subtype (ADD_FOREIGN vs ADD_INTERNAL), because foreign vocabulary is easy for lexical metrics.\n- Outputs: perturb_scores.jsonl (every row x every metric, with base_id, operator, polarity, matched_pair_id, subtype, base_status, base_endorsed), perturb_sensitivity.csv, perturb_downup.csv, coverage_perturb.csv.\n\nSTEP 6 PART C: ERROR-TYPE IDENTIFICATION (src/typing_perturb.py)\n- For each mutant with peers: medoid = nf_medoid over the peer pool under the frozen variant's agreement. Run minimal_typed_repair(mutant, medoid) (exp C repair_census.search, depth <= 2, z3-verified, 10 s budget). Predicted operator = the first op of the minimal repair, mapped to PERTURB operator names through an explicit table in the prereg (ADD_INTERNAL -> ADD for scoring, with the fine version also reported; MEANING_RENAME -> RENAME op or 'unrepairable').\n- ORACLE: the same search against the TRUE base. This separates 'the typer is weak' from 'the peer medoid is wrong'.\n- Report per operator: accuracy for peer-medoid typing, oracle typing, the flash-lite judge error_type and the local-judge type; baselines = majority class and marginal-draw chance; confusion matrices; repair-found rate. Typing counts as NOT above chance if its CI overlaps the majority baseline. If so, write the clear negative: 'no gold-free metric here identifies error type', with the oracle row showing the ceiling.\n- Output: typing.csv, typing_confusion.csv.\n\nSTEP 7 PART D: LIBRARY (src/consensus_lib.py)\n- consensus_score(text, fol, peers, mode in {align, nf, hyb, exact}) -> {c_score, n_peers, n_agree, n_unknown, per_peer verdicts}. exact = identical signature plus z3 equivalence with no mapping (the R_COMP-SIG mode).\n- graded_consensus(text, fol, peers, mode) -> {g_score, support, coverage, unit_codes}.\n- name_free_align(cand, peer, text, variant, k, tau).\n- peer_pool(text, families, k=None, dry_run=True) -> formulas: dataset E's fewshot_v1 prompt to the E generator slots via OpenRouter (env URL). It returns a cost estimate when dry_run, and it records $ for each call.\n- Re-exports equivalent_modulo_vocab (eqmv) and minimal_typed_repair.\n- Every docstring states: what it measures (agreement of a candidate formula with independent translations of the same sentence, NOT truth against a reference); inputs; outputs and their orientation (higher = more likely error); failure modes (unparseable -> score 1.0 plus status; z3 UNKNOWN never counts as agreement; fewer than 2 peers -> NA); known blind spots (NF: formula automorphisms, guarded by the tau window; ALIGN: renames; all modes: coincident errors shared by many families); cost (z3 seconds per pair, API $ per peer).\n- tests/test_consensus_lib.py: 20 known pairs (identity; full predicate rename; synonym rename; contrapositive; De Morgan; prenex; reorder; MEANING_RENAME; SWAP; NEG; QUANT; DROP; ADD; REV role swap on symmetric predicates; UNKNOWN on a forced 1 ms timeout; identical-string cache; unparseable; fewer than 2 peers; ALIGN-only match; NF-only match), each with its expected verdict per mode. peer_pool is tested with a mocked HTTP client, plus one live smoke call on 1 sentence x 2 cheap families (< $0.002).\n\nSTEP 8 OUTPUTS\n- method_out.json in exp_gen_sol_out format (aii-json validate; full/mini/preview; aii-file-size-limit split if large). ADD_FOREIGN and MEANING_RENAME use foreign vocabulary, and 69 bases are PANEL_REPAIRED, which peers may not endorse. Paired stratified Deltas with CIs: HYB - ALIGN (target within 0.01), HYB - NF, HYB - judge_local, frozen-variant - ALIGN; pooled plus long pool plus per stratum. COVERAGE VIEW: unparseable = ERROR.\\n- invariance_table.csv: rows = metric x rewrite family (REPRINT, RENAME, REORDER, DEMORGAN, CONTRAPOSITIVE, PRENEX from exp D; RENAME_SYN, RENAME_NONCE, CONTRAPOSITIVE, REORDER_COMMUTE, REORDER_QUANT, DEMORGAN from PERTURB). Columns: n_bases, FA_on_correct_bases, FA CI, flip_rate, n_flipped, source. FA and flip are in separate columns and never mixed.\\n- Over-alignment: recall at the frozen threshold on PERTURB MEANING_RENAME (DOWN/UP), PERTURB SWAP, and screen ROLE_PERMUTE / MEANING_RENAME / SWAP probes, for ALIGN, NF and HYB.\\n- NEW-AGREEMENT AUDIT on E: pairs where hyb_eq is True but align_eq is not. Report the count; the share where the candidate is a panel ERROR; and, the key precision readout, the LABEL-DISCORDANCE rate (one side CORRECT, the other ERROR under R_AB). Compare with the discordance rate among ALIGN-agreeing pairs. A higher discordance for new agreements = over-alignment, quantified on real outputs. Also draw a 30-pair sha1-ordered sample for a by-hand table in the README (candidate, peer, mapping, labels).\\n- Length: AUROC by word bin and n_conditions for ALIGN / NF / HYB (does the NF path cost more on long sentences?).\\n- Write per_item_hyb_E.jsonl (row_key, all modes, n_peers, n_unknown, fold_E), per_item_screen_hyb.jsonl, pairs_E.jsonl (sentence_id, cand row_key, peer row_key, align_eq, nf_eq, hyb_eq, nf_cost), selection.json (evidence plus the frozen variant).\\n\\nSTEP 5 PART B: PERTURB PER-TYPE SENSITIVITY (src/perturb_score.py, src/analyse_perturb.py)\\n5a Rows: 4,234 mutants (ERROR) + 868 controls (CORRECT) + the 300 unmutated bases (CORRECT; control type BASE). The BASE control measures whether peers endorse the reference at all.\\n5b Peers: for the 200 E bases, all parseable real LLM outputs of that E sentence (every family; the base is a reference with no family, so no family is left out). For the 100 R_COMP bases: consensus = NA (flagged 'no peers yet'), unless the sibling T2's rcomp_candidates.jsonl already exists at run time, in which case its FREE-condition outputs are used as a labelled secondary row. PERTURB is never scored against its own base.\\n5c Metrics scored on every row where a scorer exists without new development (scored / unscorable counts and reasons go into coverage_perturb.csv):\\n  consensus: c_align, c_nf, c_hyb, g, nf_g, g_hyb (Step 1 engine);\\n  parse_ok; pilot structural metrics (exp 6 pilot_metrics.py; reference rows);\\n  L2-bow (exp 5 l2_bow); L3 (l3_z3 against the cached E_l3_q questionnaire of the base sentence; R_COMP bases need about 100 new flash-lite questionnaires, ~$0.02, else NA);\\n  PEER+TEXT frozen (peer_text_score with exp 5 prereg fusion);\\n  SC-5 local (the mutant scored against exp 6's cached sc5_local_samples for its sentence with exp C's SC scorer; E bases only);\\n  local round-trip NLI + embedding (exp 6 roundtrip.py, local verbaliser, GPU);\\n  S4_local: exp 6 S4 feature set with ONE full-E fit (fit on all R_AB rows, coefficients saved to s4_perturb_coefs.json before PERTURB labels are read). A labelled deviation, since OOF folds do not exist for PERTURB rows.\\n  judges: local Qwen3-8B disguised and local Llama-3.1-8B disguised (GPU, exp 6 local_llm prompts); judge_cheap_disg = gemini-2.5-flash-lite, rubric A (exp D judges.py verbatim), disguise = dataset 3's per-sentence bijection. About 5.4k calls, ~$0.35, cap $1.0. Estimate from a 20-row pilot first. Order: E-base rows first, then R_COMP-base rows; the type label is kept for Part C.\\n5d Thresholds, frozen in prereg_perturb.json before any PERTURB statistic: PRIMARY = each metric's matched FA = 0.10 threshold on E R_AB LLM CORRECT rows (with fractional ties). This is uniform because every metric has E scores in per_item_E / E_baseline_features / Step 2; for the consensus variants the Step 3 screen thresholds are also reported. SECONDARY = FA 0.10 on the PERTURB non-rename controls (in-sample and labelled as such).\\n5e Statistics per operator x polarity (QUANT/REV/RESTR are UP-only; SCOPE/UNGLUE are reported as absent with 0 rows; MOVE (6) is reported and not analysed):\\n  recall at the frozen threshold, with a base-clustered CI;\\n  BASE-MATCHED AUROC = mutants of the operator vs controls (non-rename controls + BASE) from the SAME bases, averaged over bases (within-base AUROC), plus the pooled version;\\n  DOWN-vs-UP on the 1,354 matched pairs: the paired detection difference (flag_DOWN - flag_UP), its base-clustered bootstrap CI and McNemar counts, plus the paired score difference. Prediction written in the prereg: equivalence-based consensus is polarity-symmetric (|diff| < 0.05), while lexical, NLI and judge metrics may miss DOWN (restrictor-side) edits more often;\\n  breakdowns by base reference_status (TRUSTED_AGREED / GOLD_PANEL_OK / PANEL_REPAIRED / R_COMP) AND by BASE ENDORSEMENT (the base's own c_hyb < 1, i.e. at least one peer agrees, vs no peer agrees). Consensus can only detect a mutant of a base that peers endorse, so this split is the correct denominator for interpreting consensus recall. Report both, and the share of bases endorsed;\\n  INVARIANCE/SENSITIVITY TRADE-OFF per metric: the point (RENAME_SYN FA, MEANING_RENAME recall), plus the same with RENAME_NONCE, in one table and one figure spec. A metric must be low on the first axis and high on the second;\\n  ADD split by subtype (ADD_FOREIGN vs ADD_INTERNAL), because foreign vocabulary is easy for lexical metrics.\\n- Outputs: perturb_scores.jsonl (every row x every metric, with base_id, operator, polarity, matched_pair_id, subtype, base_status, base_endorsed), perturb_sensitivity.csv, perturb_downup.csv, coverage_perturb.csv.\\n\\nSTEP 6 PART C: ERROR-TYPE IDENTIFICATION (src/typing_perturb.py)\\n- For each mutant with peers: medoid = nf_medoid over the peer pool under the frozen variant's agreement. Run minimal_typed_repair(mutant, medoid) (exp C repair_census.search, depth <= 2, z3-verified, 10 s budget). Predicted operator = the first op of the minimal repair, mapped to PERTURB operator names through an explicit table in the prereg (ADD_INTERNAL -> ADD for scoring, with the fine version also reported; MEANING_RENAME -> RENAME op or 'unrepairable').\\n- ORACLE: the same search against the TRUE base. This separates 'the typer is weak' from 'the peer medoid is wrong'.\\n- Report per operator: accuracy for peer-medoid typing, oracle typing, the flash-lite judge error_type and the local-judge type; baselines = majority class and marginal-draw chance; confusion matrices; repair-found rate. Typing counts as NOT above chance if its CI overlaps the majority baseline. If so, write the clear negative: 'no gold-free metric here identifies error type', with the oracle row showing the ceiling.\\n- Output: typing.csv, typing_confusion.csv.\\n\\nSTEP 7 PART D: LIBRARY (src/consensus_lib.py)\\n- consensus_score(text, fol, peers, mode in {align, nf, hyb, exact}) -> {c_score, n_peers, n_agree, n_unknown, per_peer verdicts}. exact = identical signature plus z3 equivalence with no mapping (the R_COMP-SIG mode).\\n- graded_consensus(text, fol, peers, mode) -> {g_score, support, coverage, unit_codes}.\\n- name_free_align(cand, peer, text, variant, k, tau).\\n- peer_pool(text, families, k=None, dry_run=True) -> formulas: dataset E's fewshot_v1 prompt to the E generator slots via OpenRouter (env URL). It returns a cost estimate when dry_run, and it records $ for each call.\\n- Re-exports equivalent_modulo_vocab (eqmv) and minimal_typed_repair.\\n- Every docstring states: what it measures (agreement of a candidate formula with independent translations of the same sentence, NOT truth against a reference); inputs; outputs and their orientation (higher = more likely error); failure modes (unparseable -> score 1.0 plus status; z3 UNKNOWN never counts as agreement; fewer than 2 peers -> NA); known blind spots (NF: formula automorphisms, guarded by the tau window; ALIGN: renames; all modes: coincident errors shared by many families); cost (z3 seconds per pair, API $ per peer).\\n- tests/test_consensus_lib.py: 20 known pairs (identity; full predicate rename; synonym rename; contrapositive; De Morgan; prenex; reorder; MEANING_RENAME; SWAP; NEG; QUANT; DROP; ADD; REV role swap on symmetric predicates; UNKNOWN on a forced 1 ms timeout; identical-string cache; unparseable; fewer than 2 peers; ALIGN-only match; NF-only match), each with its expected verdict per mode. peer_pool is tested with a mocked HTTP client, plus one live smoke call on 1 sentence x 2 cheap families (< $0.002).\\n\\nSTEP 8 OUTPUTS\\n- method_out.json in exp_gen_sol_out format (aii-json validate; full/mini/preview; aii-file-size-limit split if large). One example per E row, PERTURB row and rewrite row. metadata_fold in {E, PERTURB, PERTURB_CONTROL, PERTURB_BASE, REWRITE_EXPD, SCREEN}; input = blind text + candidate_fol; output = label; predict_c_score_hyb, predict_g_score_hyb, predict_c_score_nf, predict_c_score_align, predict_frozen_invariant (the selected variant), and on PERTURB rows predict_<every Part B metric>; metadata_operator, metadata_polarity, metadata_base_endorsed.\\n- results/: invariance_table.csv, perturb_sensitivity.csv, perturb_downup.csv, typing.csv, selection.json, prereg_hyb.json(+sha256), prereg_perturb.json(+sha256), deviations.json, cost_ledger.jsonl, tables.md, figure specs (trade-off scatter; recall heatmap operator x polarity x metric), README.md with the headline verdicts, .aii/manifest.yaml (delete regenerable .venv*/ and the sqlite pair cache if > 10 MB, with regeneration commands).\",\n\"fallback_plan\": \"(1) REGRESSION GATE FAILS (re-derived c_score_align / nf_c_score differ from frozen on > 1% of rows): diagnose parser/eqmv version, timeouts and the family map first. If the cause is timeouts only, rerun those pairs with pair_cap 60 s. If it is unresolved after 30 min, compute HYB as OR over the pair verdicts that DO reproduce, list mismatched rows as a separate stratum, and state that HYB headline numbers carry that caveat. Never overwrite frozen columns. (2) z3/NF TOO SLOW (projected E > 120 min after the 100-sentence stage): NF k 5 -> 3; then unit cap 16 -> 10; then score L25 on the original-200 sentences only (top-up rows are untestable alone). PERTURB consensus is scored E-bases first, so a cut drops R_COMP-base rows (which have no peers anyway) before E-base rows. (3) NEITHER VARIANT ELIGIBLE: freeze nothing; report per-source rename FA for both and name which rename source breaks each (nonce renames are expected to be the hard case, because anchors fail). Iteration 4 then reads ALIGN on R_COMP. Do NOT relax the 0.10 bar or tune tau. (4) THRESHOLD DEGENERATE even with fractional ties (e.g. > 50% of CORRECT tie at the max): report threshold-free AUROC and the tie share, and mark recall rows 'threshold-degenerate' instead of reporting 0. (5) KEY DOWN / 402 / daily limit: finish all CPU/GPU parts; poll every 15 min until 00:00 UTC if the run window allows. Otherwise flash-lite on PERTURB falls back to a pre-declared subsample (all MEANING_RENAME, SWAP, NEG, DROP, RENAME_SYN, RENAME_NONCE and BASE rows first, then the others in sha1 order), and the local Qwen3-8B disguised judge becomes the judge row with a label. Never switch the API judge model. (6) BUDGET: hard stop $2.0. If the 20-row pilot projects the flash-lite PERTURB arm > $1.0, apply the subsample in (5). (7) GPU unavailable or OOM: halve the batch size (aii-parallel-computing OOM fallback); if still failing, drop local round-trip first, then the Llama judge, keeping Qwen3-8B. List each dropped metric in coverage_perturb.csv as 'not run: reason'. (8) Too few peers for PERTURB E bases (sentences with < 2 parseable peers): consensus NA for those bases, counted in coverage; no substitution. (9) Operator cells with < 30 rows after unscorable exclusions: report counts and point estimates, mark them untestable, and give no CI claims. (10) Typing search times out on > 30% of mutants: raise the budget to 20 s on a 500-row sha1 sample and report typing on that sample only. (11) If HYB fails the 0.01 AUROC target or rename FA, report it exactly as pre-registered. No post-hoc variants (e.g. weighted OR, other tau) enter the headline; any exploration is labelled post hoc in deviations.json.\",\n\"testing_plan\": \"UNIT (before any data run): tests/test_consensus_lib.py (the 20 known pairs x modes listed in Step 7, including ALIGN-only and NF-only matches proving the OR, UNKNOWN never counted as agreement, identical-string True, unparseable -> 1.0 + status, fewer than 2 peers -> NA). tests/test_threshold.py: fractional tie-breaking gives FA exactly 0.10 on a synthetic vector where 40% of negatives tie at the max; it must reproduce exp 5's posthoc NF-anchored numbers (RENAME FA ~0.074, ROLE_PERMUTE recall ~0.77) on the screen from the cached screen scores. tests/test_isolation.py: the scoring scripts never read label keys (grep); analyse_* refuse without a prereg sha256 older than the score files. REGRESSION: (a) the Step 2c gate (re-derived c_score_align and nf_c_score match frozen on >= 99% of E rows); (b) screen c_score_align reproduces exp C track-L AUROC 0.866 +- 0.005; (c) re-scoring 30 exp D rewrite rows with the vendored judge hits the cache and costs $0; (d) the flash-lite PERTURB pilot on 20 rows gives < 2% parse failures and a cost per call within 30% of the estimate. SANITY SIGNALS: identical-string and z3-equivalent controls (CONTRAPOSITIVE, REORDER_*, DEMORGAN) must give hyb_eq True with the base's endorsers in >= 95% of cases; otherwise the rewrite/parse path is broken. c_hyb <= min(c_align, c_nf) row-wise by construction (assert). Placebo: E labels shuffled within stratum give stratified AUROC ~0.5 for c_hyb. PERTURB: mutants of NON-endorsed bases should give recall close to the control FA for consensus (expected, not a bug), so check that the base-endorsement split shows it. STAGED SCALE: mini (20 E sentences + 50 PERTURB rows) -> 100 sentences / 500 PERTURB rows -> all, logging seconds per pair, UNKNOWN share (< 5% on CTRL expected) and peers per row. POST-HOC AUDIT: tests/audit_rederive.py recomputes the HYB - ALIGN stratified Delta and three PERTURB recall cells through a minimal independent sklearn/numpy path (agreement to 1e-9), and checks that every paired comparison used identical n.\",\n\"domain_practice\": \"What I read for this step: the neurosymbolic handbook (aii-handbook-auto-neurosymbolic, 2026-07). Its rules: FOLIO/MALLS gold as shipped is ~36-39% wrong (2606.02837), so labels here are dataset E's panel/solver tiers and PERTURB's construction labels; compiling or being provable is not faithfulness; gold-free round-trip certification is an occupied lane (2604.25031) and is only a baseline; there is no agreed gold-free faithfulness metric. Also: dataset 3's card (PERTURB construction, operator counts, the explicit instruction to analyse RENAME_SYN and RENAME_NONCE separately and never pool PERTURB with real errors), exp 5's peer_text.py/pool_scoring.py/prereg (the ALIGN and NF definitions, tau 0.5, the 1.000000001 tie-degenerate thresholds), the iteration-3 strategy, and the sibling research plan's verified neighbours (NoTB 2608.21962 cross-model formal consensus; GenV 2609.11085; Li et al. 2410.20936; Kim et al. 2506.07962 on correlated errors). No new web lookups in this step. The metric meta-evaluation norms below are the strategist's standing-knowledge list (FRANK/Goyal and Durrett for per-type error sensitivity with synthetic-for-real caveats; CheckList-style invariance/directional tests; TRUE/SummaC for threshold-free AUROC; WMT for paired, clustered comparisons). EXPECTED PRACTICE: (1) invariance measured as FALSE ALARMS on meaning-preserving rewrites of CORRECT inputs, separately per rewrite type, not as score-flip rates; (2) per-error-type sensitivity from synthetic perturbations with known labels, reported per type and never averaged into a real-error AUROC, because synthetic error distributions misstate real performance (Goyal and Durrett 2021); (3) matched false-alarm operating points fixed on development data, with ties handled explicitly for quantized scores; (4) resampling clustered by the unit that shares information (sentence on E; base formula on PERTURB, since mutants of a base share peers and controls); (5) directional (polarity) tests with matched pairs, as in monotonicity/NLI stress tests; (6) every metric the practitioner would use (LLM judge, SC, round-trip, lexical, fused stack) on the same rows; (7) an over-alignment/precision check for any matcher made more lenient, because loosening equivalence trades invariance for missed errors; (8) coverage in the denominator.\",\n\"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS: FA and flip reported in separate columns per rewrite source, with RENAME_SYN / RENAME_NONCE / exp D RENAME kept apart. PERTURB is analysed per operator x polarity only, never pooled with E AUROC. Thresholds are frozen on development data with fractional tie-breaking, which fixes iteration 2's tie artefact. Base-clustered and sentence-clustered bootstraps are used. Matched DOWN/UP pairs give a directional test with a pre-stated prediction. Every locally or cheaply available metric is scored on identical PERTURB rows, with coverage counted. Over-alignment is measured three ways: synthetic MEANING_RENAME/SWAP/ROLE_PERMUTE recall, and label-discordance of new agreements on REAL E pairs, the last being a direct precision check of the loosened matcher on real outputs. A regression gate ties recomputed consensus to the frozen exp 5 values before anything is built on it. The selection rule is fixed and written before labels are joined, and R_COMP stays unseen for the confirmation read by the sibling. The library is documented with measured failure modes. DEPARTS: (a) E is DEVELOPMENT data for choosing the invariant variant, so this artifact's E AUROC for the chosen variant is optimistically selected (between only 2 candidates). Cost: the held-out claim for HYB rests on the sibling's R_COMP read. (b) PERTURB bases are references, not model outputs, and the mutants are synthetic. ADD_FOREIGN and MEANING_RENAME use foreign vocabulary, and 69 bases are PANEL_REPAIRED, which peers may not endorse. Cost: per-type recall is a sensitivity profile, not an estimate of real-error detection. Mitigated by the base-endorsement and base-status splits. (c) R_COMP bases have no peers yet, so consensus on 100 of 300 bases is NA, while the other metrics score them. Cross-metric comparisons are therefore also reported on the E-base subset where every metric exists. (d) S4_local on PERTURB uses one full-E fit instead of OOF folds (a labelled deviation). (e) Frontier and API SC/round-trip arms are not run on PERTURB (the budget belongs to T1); only flash-lite represents API judges. (f) The typing readout uses a minimal-repair search whose operator vocabulary does not cover every PERTURB operator (e.g. MEANING_RENAME, CONN). Those are reported as uncodable, and the oracle row bounds the typer. (g) No fresh literature lookups in this planning step.\"\n}\n", "structuredPatch": [], "originalFile": null, "userModified": false}

File: /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_u75jRHUss0zo/3_invention_loop/iter_3/gen_plan/gen_plan_experiment_3/README.md

# gen_plan_experiment_3 (iteration 3): rename-invariant consensus (PEER_HYB) + PERTURB per-type sensitivity + consensus library

This directory holds a PLAN for strategy slot T3+T5; a separate executor carries it out.

## Layout
- `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`: the plan. Fields: title, summary, runpod_compute_profile, builds_on, implementation_pseudocode, fallback_plan, testing_plan, domain_practice, practice_alignment.
- `.aii/manifest.yaml`: empty; this step creates no heavy files.

## Plan in one paragraph
- **Definition.** PEER_HYB counts two formulas as agreeing when either of two frozen maps yields a z3-verified equivalence:
  - the exp-5 ALIGN map (iteration-1 `eqmv`), or
  - the exp-5 NF-anchored map (tau 0.5).
- **Development.** It is computed on the screen and on all of E, where E serves as development data.
- **Regression gate.** Before anything else, the executor must reproduce the frozen `c_score_align` and `nf_c_score` on ≥99% of rows.
- **Invariance.** It is measured as the FALSE-ALARM rate on three rename sources, kept separate:
  - exp D RENAME;
  - PERTURB RENAME_SYN;
  - PERTURB RENAME_NONCE.
- **Selection.** A rule fixed in `prereg_hyb.json` freezes HYB or NF-anchored. It uses matched-FA with fractional tie-breaking. R_COMP is never seen: the sibling T2 scores both variants on R_COMP.
- **PERTURB.** Every available metric is scored on 4,234 mutants, 868 controls and 300 unmutated bases. Results are reported per operator × polarity:
  - recall at frozen FA 0.10;
  - AUROC matched within each base;
  - the DOWN-vs-UP difference on matched pairs;
  - a split by whether the base is endorsed by its peers.

  These rows are never pooled with real errors.
- **Typing.** Error types come from a minimal repair against the peer medoid, compared with an oracle repair against the true base and with chance.
- **Library.** `src/consensus_lib.py`, with docstrings and 20 known-pair unit tests.
- **Budget.** At most $2 of API spend (about $0.6 expected).

## Notes
- The input prompt for this planning step arrived truncated. The task was reconstructed from the iteration-3 strategy (`../../gen_strat/gen_strat_1/.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`, artifact_directions[2]).
- Nothing here needs to be run.
```

Plan written 2026-09-24 at iter_3/gen_plan/gen_plan_experiment_3 of run_u75jRHUss0zo. The input prompt arrived truncated; the task was reconstructed from the GenStrat I3 artifact_directions[2]. See [[project_nl2fol_faithfulness_genstrat_iter3_run_u75jRHUss0zo]].

- **HYB needs pair-level verdicts.** Exp 5's per_item_E.jsonl holds only aggregate c_score_align (via vendor_c common.eqmv in pool_scoring.py) and nf_c_score. The OR must therefore be recomputed per pair, and the plan gates it: the re-derived values must match the frozen ones on ≥99% of rows.
- **Thresholds.** Exp 5's prereg thresholds of 1.000000001 are the tie artefact; the plan mandates fractional tie-breaking.
- **Selection.** Tie → NF-anchored. If neither variant is eligible, nothing is frozen and R_COMP is read with ALIGN.
- **PERTURB.**
  - The 100 R_COMP bases have no peers, so their consensus is NA.
  - Recall is split by whether peers endorse the base.
  - Thresholds are set at FA 0.10 on E R_AB CORRECT rows, the same rule for every metric.
  - The DOWN/UP prediction is that consensus is polarity-symmetric.
- **Precision of the loosened matcher.** Check label discordance among HYB-only agreements on real E pairs.

**Why:** these are the traps the executor would otherwise hit.
**How to apply:** when executing or reviewing, check the regression gate, the fractional ties, and that prereg_hyb is dated before the label join.
```

### [156] ASSISTANT · 2026-09-24 01:31:44 UTC

```
I've written the plan for experiment slot 3, but your message arrived cut off: only its last lines reached me, and the pilot study you appended didn't come through. I rebuilt the task from the iteration-3 strategy on disk. Its third direction is the rename-invariant consensus (PEER_HYB), per-error-type sensitivity on the synthetic PERTURB suite, and the consensus library. If you meant a different task, or want the pilot study to shape it, paste them again. For now the pilot metrics are scored on PERTURB as reference rows only.

**What the plan does**
- **Part A: rename-invariant consensus.** Two translations count as agreeing if either the name-based alignment (ALIGN) or the name-free alignment anchored in the sentence (NF-anchored) proves them equivalent with z3. The variant is scored on the screen items and on all of E.
  - Iteration 2 saved only per-row totals, so the executor has to recompute each candidate–peer pair. A check comes first: the recomputed scores must match the saved ones on at least 99% of rows.
  - False alarms on renames are measured separately for three sources: exp D RENAME, PERTURB RENAME_SYN and PERTURB RENAME_NONCE. They are never mixed with flip rate.
  - A selection rule is written into `prereg_hyb.json` before E labels are joined. It uses the 10% false-alarm cut-off with ties split fractionally, which fixes the iteration-2 tie artefact. R_COMP is never seen, so the sibling R_COMP experiment gives the independent confirmation.
  - Over-matching is checked on real E pairs: are pairs that only the new rule matches more often labelled differently (one correct, one wrong)?
- **Part B: per-error-type sensitivity.** Every available metric is scored on 4,234 mutants, 868 controls and the 300 unmutated bases, reported per operator × polarity. Results are never pooled with real errors. Three additions:
  - results split by whether peers accept the unmutated base at all, since consensus can't catch mutants of a base no peer agrees with;
  - a paired test of edits at the two polarity positions (DOWN vs UP), predicting consensus scores both about the same;
  - a table per metric of rename false alarms against MEANING_RENAME recall.
- **Part C: error-type identification.** Peer-based typing is compared with chance, with the judge's type label, and with a ceiling from repairing against the true base.
- **Part D: the library.** `src/consensus_lib.py` gets full docstrings and unit tests on 20 known pairs.
- **Budget:** API spend is capped at $2, with about $0.6 expected.

I checked the plan file's JSON structure by pattern search only, since there's no shell here to parse it.

Files are in `gen_plan_experiment_3/`:
- `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`
- `README.md`
- `.aii/manifest.yaml`
```
